### Volume 06, Batch 0005 — review repairs, 28 September 2026, against `logs/batch-0005.review.log`

**THE REVIEW RETURNED FIX ON ELEVEN REQUIRED FINDINGS AND FOUR PROSE IMPROVEMENTS. ALL ELEVEN REQUIRED FINDINGS ARE REPAIRED AND THREE OF THE FOUR PROSE IMPROVEMENTS ARE TAKEN. THE REPAIR RESTARTED NOTHING, REWROTE NO CHAPTER, CHANGED NO PLOT, MOVED NO DATE AND NO COST, AND EVERY WITHDRAWN FIGURE IS NAMED BELOW AND AT `state/continuity.md` § *Batch 0005 (Volume 06) review repairs*.**

1. **CH 291 — the private hire is closed inside itself.** A man of about fifty-five hired the yard to look under his own boat and stated the question — whether the thing along her starboard side is his or was there before he bought her — and no page answered it. **The chapter now answers him: a cut in the rock about nine feet long and about two inches across, with dressed sides and square at both ends, counted twice, in about four feet of that boat's own ground; not his, cut before she was built, and not attributable to anybody, and entered in the first column because a man can go and look at it with a pan and a line and not because anybody knows what it is; and he pays the whole of it without arguing with a figure and does not ask what it meant and does not ask to have his boat up again.** The six hundred and forty-nine pence and its three components are untouched. **The chapter's fourth-column line also now reads *a man who saw one more than sixty years ago***, the thirty years having dragged the Crown Seal's first sighting forward, which is the one thing this batch's reveal discipline turns on.
2. **CH 292, CH 293 AND CH 296 — the standing far-bench sentence now reads *a man of sixty-one at a far bench* in all ten chapters.** The earlier repair put it into seven and left three in the ambiguous form, and in Chapter 292 a man of sixty-one is on the page all day in a boat. Chapter 292 also gained the closing gift clause every other day carries.
3. **CH 293 — the storm's second hand and page is in a man's own book four streets off the front of that harbor, and not in a boat a mile and a quarter off the mole,** which the chapter had said twice on its own face and which Chapter 295, Chapter 300 and the state files always said. A mile and a quarter is the standing distance from the head of that mole to the front. **The crate is also about nine feet from the two objects of nobody's and they are no longer said to have shifted,** and the eight feet in Chapter 295 is nine.
4. **CH 295, CH 296 AND CH 299 — *about four hundred years* is withdrawn.** `bible/world.md` dates the Quietening at three hundred and twelve years, so a Crown-cut sill is **about three hundred years old**, and that is the figure in Chapters 297 and 298; in Chapter 299 the figure is gone entirely, having been attached to a remark about a shackle where it meant nothing. **The two surviving *in a year and a half* of elapsed time in Chapters 296 and 299 are *six months*,** the first telling having been on the night of Sunday the thirteenth of November. The house's *in about four years* look-forwards are untouched in the six places that carry one, and two of the eight that had been carrying one no longer do.
5. **CH 296 — the mangled sentence at the emotional peak of the volume's most intimate scene now reads that there is no second part of the true answer,** which is an evasion that reads as one and lets the reply that it is a very good way of putting it land.
6. **CH 297 — the first column no longer puts the letting in a boat between half past eleven and one in the afternoon.** The exchange is on the stone of the mole steps from about eleven until the boat goes out at about half past eleven, the ship's hand asking for it from the boat, nothing written on the day and the whole of it entered at about half past nine that night with all three of them at the bench; and the second column says *nobody on that mole* and not *nobody in that boat*. **The decision, its price, the sentence and the woman's other half are untouched.**
7. **CH 298 — the quoted order now reads *Four. The masters are not to be asked which of them made it*,** the sheet being a thing anybody can check and Chapter 300 quoting it correctly, and his reply is now *you had the fourth one wrong*, so that the wrongness is hers and not the document's.
8. **CH 300 — the read-back is *a hundred and twelve* and not *a hundred and eleven*,** and the final image no longer says a round was cut out of the plate that has closed. **THE ROUND IS A SEPARATE COLLAR-MOUNTED DISC THAT CAME OFF THE FLOOR OF THE ROOM AND IS BEDDED IN A SEPARATE PIECE OF WORKED GLASS, AND THE PLATE IS STILL IN THE SILL AND WENT DOWN INTO THE REBATE ON THE ELEVENTH.** *A piece cut out of the plate that has closed* and *the plate and the piece are one thing* are withdrawn wherever they stood. **AND THE TWO DAYS OF PLANT AND AIR AT FOURTEEN MARKS, THREE SHILLINGS AND FOURPENCE ARE TWENTY-EIGHT MARKS, SIX SHILLINGS AND EIGHTPENCE, BEING FOUR THOUSAND FIVE HUNDRED AND SIXTY PENCE,** and the earlier figure of three shillings and eightpence is withdrawn; no chapter states the total. Chapter 300's doubled preposition and its *in about four days' time* are also gone, the latter being the eighteenth of May.
9. **PRE-EXISTING AND FLAGGED FOR THE VOLUME AUDIT, NOT TOUCHED BY A PROSE PHASE: `bible/characters.md` still gives Tobias Wren as fifty-eight, and `outline/volume-06.md` L28 and all ten chapters use sixty-one.**

### The volume's shape after fifty chapters of it

**VOLUME 06 IS WRITTEN. Chapters 251 to 300, fifty consecutive days from Tuesday the sixteenth of March to Friday the fourteenth of May, the first ten of them two days to a chapter and the last forty one day to a chapter. NO CHAPTER OF VOLUME 06 IS OWED BY THIS PHASE, NO VOLUME-CLOSE OR AUDIT PHASE EXISTS, AND NONE WAS CREATED, AND NO VOLUME 07 OUTLINE EXISTS IN THIS REPOSITORY AND NONE WAS INVENTED.** The plate in eleven feet is in eleven feet with its note open for a third volume. The five bells are on nobody's schedule. The delayed note is at eleven and two. The third column is empty and unappointed. The forty marks stand twice. Five pages are in five drawers. **A working harbor of that coast is poorer on the last page than on the first, and the last page says so in a numbered thirteen, and the fourth of the thirteen is a seal line and the thirteenth is the poverty.**

## Volume 07, Batch 0001 (Chapters 301–310) — complete. Movement 1, a notice on a wall that was bare at eight, and a copy nobody asked for. **THE MOVEMENT'S TURN IS PAID. THE VOLUME IS NOT CLOSED.**

**`outline/volume-07.md` WAS CREATED BY THE PHASE THAT WROTE THIS BATCH, ON 28 SEPTEMBER 2026, BUILT FROM `outline/series.md` AND `outline/ending.md` AND THE FIFTY CHAPTERS OF VOLUME 06 AS WRITTEN AND THE FOUR STATE FILES, WITH THIRTY-ONE NUMBERED LOCKS AND TWO LETTERED LOCKS OF ITS OWN, L1a AND L27a, WHICH ARE THIRTY-THREE HEADED LOCKS IN ALL. NO CARD FILE EXISTS FOR ANY VOLUME 07 BATCH AND NONE WAS INVENTED FOR ANY OTHER, AND THE BATCH PROMPT SAYS SO IN ITS OWN FIRST LINE. ITS LOCK L1 FIXES THE GRID BY RULE AND BY ONE ANCHOR — THE FOURTEENTH OF MAY IS A FRIDAY — AND PRINTS NO DAY TABLE. THE VOLUME'S DIVERGENCE FROM `outline/series.md` IS ON FIVE LOAD-BEARING LINES AND IS FLAGGED AT ITS OWN § *Divergences* WITH THE LINE NUMBERS, AND NEITHER FILE WAS EDITED.**

**Batch 0001 as written: 28,902 words across Chapters 301 to 310, RE-MEASURED AFTER THE REVIEW REPAIRS OF 28 SEPTEMBER 2026 AGAINST `logs/next-0001.review.log` (Ch301 2,474 · Ch302 2,845 · Ch303 3,198 · Ch304 2,483 · Ch305 2,970 · Ch306 2,351 · Ch307 2,676 · Ch308 2,846 · Ch309 2,624 · Ch310 4,435), measured with `wc -w` including headings, against the 2,200–3,200 guideline. TEN CONSECUTIVE DAYS FROM SATURDAY THE FIFTEENTH OF MAY TO MONDAY THE TWENTY-FOURTH, ONE DAY TO A CHAPTER, NO DAY MISSING AND NO DAY IN TWO CHAPTERS. NINE CHAPTERS ARE INSIDE THE GUIDELINE AND ONE IS PAST IT, being Chapter 310, the close, at one thousand two hundred and thirty-five words past it; it carries the read-back of four columns, three statements, a stranger's scene and the whole standing inventory once, which is the same shape as Chapter 300 and Chapter 280. **EVERY COUNTED FIGURE IN THE TEN CHAPTERS NOW COUNTS, after the repairs of 28 September 2026: the chandler's answer is nine words, the man on the tender's answer to the boat's man is nine words, the eighth clause's courtesy is thirteen words, the boy's message is nine words and not six, the man of about thirty-four names four of the notice's lines and not three and a half, and the slate in Chapter 306 is headed *FEBRUARY. THE NINETEENTH.* and is priced in pence first per lock L5.** NO SCENE WAS CUT AND NO COST WAS DROPPED TO BRING ANY CHAPTER INSIDE A NUMBER, AND NO PERCENTAGE OF APPARATUS IS QUOTED, because the figures the last batches carried under that name were measured on different definitions and this file already rules that a percentage re-measured on a different definition is a different figure wearing the same name. **THE REPAIRS OF 28 SEPTEMBER 2026 ADDED 862 WORDS TO THE BATCH AND THE FIGURES ABOVE ARE THE REPAIRED ONES; THE PRE-REPAIR FIGURES WERE 28,040 AND ARE NOT THE FIGURES OF THE CHAPTERS AS THEY NOW STAND.**

### The chapter summaries

**Chapter 301 — Saturday the fifteenth of May. A crate on the mole steps for a fourth day, the last of six hundred and sixty pence going into one front, and a tender of about sixty tons lying off the south end from ten at night until first light.** A crate under a tarpaulin that about nine people a day walk past; a glazier of about forty-four on a street four streets off the front who has paid a boy of nineteen every Friday since the sixth of March and is not going to be the man who does not; a boat's owner of about fifty-five up the hill at about eleven asking whether a man is coming to his boat on the slip on Monday, and told, twice and in the same words, why this yard is not going to go and look; and at about half past four the man in a blue coat who has been, a line on the ring bolt, a book on the tail of her, a minute looking at the water with his face to it, and no question asked of anybody. **Cost:** a boat's week and a copper pipe, on a slip at three shillings a week, and a rest out of a man who has not slept since Tuesday. **Opens:** the tender, and the refusal that is going to be the volume's method. Nothing came in and nothing went out.

**Chapter 302 — Sunday the sixteenth of May. A shipwright of about sixty, a copper pipe at Ternwatch, three shillings a week for a slip, and a boat's man of about forty who went out to a tender at five and came back at half past five with nine words in his mouth.** A green length of copper under a housing that wants replacing and a day's water with about four hours in a month; a chandler who gave nine words and no list on Saturday and says out loud that the same nine words would have been given on any Tuesday in the year, and who can no longer be certain which of eleven boats he could have named was out; a tender of about sixty tons lying off the north side with about forty people on the mole; a boat's man of about forty sent out in a dinghy and back in an hour and a quarter with nothing in his head but a question and an answer. **Cost:** nine words in nine people's hearing, and the boat's man counted them because he came off the water with nothing else in it. **Opens:** *then you will want to hear it again from the man who wrote it*, and the rule that a man who does not tell owns a piece of an answer and this yard has no standing to take it off him.

**Chapter 303 — Monday the seventeenth of May. The fifty-fourth time that wall came down, the forty-seventh charge walked on none, a crate going up that coast on the deck of a coal boat, and a notice going up at nine on a wall that was bare at eight.** The wall bare at eight; a crate up a hundred and forty feet of stone on a woman's own back with her reason given once on the steps and not again; **eight clauses of the closed-sea squadron put up at about a quarter past nine over the top of a sheet, with two naval notices already on that wall that this yard did not take down**; a reading in a market in about forty people's hearing by a man of about thirty-four who says he cannot tell what four of the lines mean and will not guess; a woman of about fifty-eight with the end stall saying the sentence of the whole notice, being that a closure has a door in it and this has got a door in the sixth line and no handle on the inside; a barrel of lamp oil at thirty-one shillings and sixpence in February and a mole-head light out in October. **Cost:** a crate this yard cannot reach, and a question asked in a market — *where is that?* — with no answer. **Opens:** the first clause's missing office.

**Chapter 304 — Tuesday the eighteenth of May. The thirtieth week with no envelope, a clerk with a quarter owed him for two months, and a boat of about seventy tons that went out at eleven without a piece of paper.** **A wage Tuesday, four marks not paid, and thirty weeks fallen due.** A properly made declaration, made before two witnesses at a desk in Ternwatch, carried to the west basin and refused by a clerk of about sixty who has been a clerk twenty-two years, for three true reasons, ending with the words *I do not know* out loud in a room with one man in it, and a request that the man not decide about his boat there. Then the boat goes out at eleven with no declaration, is not asked, not stopped, not followed, lies off the south end four hours and comes in at six and takes her berth. **Cost:** a Tuesday on which nothing happened, in a harbor that will hold a record of it that nobody can check. **Opens:** the two true sentences four lines apart, and the round of glass in a house four days up the coast.

**Chapter 305 — Wednesday the nineteenth of May. A man with two marks and one shilling and nine days on a prescription, one mark and eightpence and a penny of line, three soundings inside the harbor line, and a mark fouled to about a foot and seven inches.** **The far bench occupied from ten until four and not approached.** A plasterer of about thirty-eight asking for a boat to carry a chest up that coast for his wife, in about nine people's hearing, with his figure said out loud, and refused with the reason in two parts: a party with no instrument cannot give a hirer a paper to produce, and a man of this yard carrying a thing for a person in a house makes this yard a man in that house's business. Then the yard's last hireable money: **a boat of about thirty tons, a mark and eightpence, a penny of line, on this yard's own account, and three soundings, and the mark on the face of the second seam fouled about a foot and seven inches on the weather side and nothing on the other, and sand under the crown and not rock.** **Cost:** two marks and one shilling against eight marks and a day, and a boat that is not going to be repaired, and nine days on a chest that ran out about a week before the chapter. **Opens:** the fouling ladder, which is in a man's own book and which nobody is going to explain.

**Chapter 306 — Thursday the twentieth of May. The forty-eighth charge walked on none, nine things on the back of a slate in a fish market, and a tenth figure that is the price of a sheet of paper.** **The cistern charged and walked on none.** Nine February figures, none of them a quotation, none of them set in that market, every one of them off a manifest out of an agent at Ternwatch whose price is set in a city four hundred miles off by people who have never come to this quay; and then the tenth figure, a sheet of paper at a penny and forty for a shilling, which has not moved in nine years, and one person in that market asked to look at it. And in the shed that night, a question about the woman of about fifty-eight with a drawer under her bed, refused, on the ground that this yard has one address in this country, **with the reason given as worse than in April and not better, because in April the yard did not know her and now the yard knows she is frightened.** **Cost:** a second address, considered twice in sixteen days, for two different reasons, both of them somebody else's.

**Chapter 307 — Friday the twenty-first of May. Eleven boats of the herring third lay lying off with about fourteen men in them, fourpence a candle, and a man of about sixty of the first lay who has three things and will not say the rest of them in a shop.** Eleven boats inside the harbor line doing nothing wrong; a candle burned and not earned; a chandler who says he has not lost a customer, they are out there; **and a man of about sixty of the first lay, forty-one years on that water, who asked a question in this harbor twice and was answered neither time, saying that he has had three things since Tuesday, naming three honest targets — a man with a book, a chandler, and the harbor authority of this harbor — and saying all three are wrong, AND THEN STOPPING ON PURPOSE AND SAYING WHY OUT LOUD, being that a sentence said in a chandler's on a Friday morning is in eleven markets by Sunday and comes back with a man's name on it that the man did not put there, and that he had watched that happen twice on this coast and once it was fair and once it was murder and he could not tell afterwards which was which.** He is asked for a name and has not got one and will not go and look, and says the correct number of times to ask a question is three and not four. He takes two candles at fourpence and goes down to the water and says he will come back into that shop on Monday. A boy of seventeen carries nine words up the hill from the *Anna Ray* and is taken up the front street instead. **Cost:** eleven boats, fourteen men, and a sentence that was not said. **THE VOLUME'S GOVERNING SENTENCE AND THE CAPTAIN ARE NOT ON THIS PAGE AND ARE MOVEMENT 2'S; the first draft of this chapter spent both and they are withdrawn.**

**Chapter 308 — Saturday the twenty-second of May. The far bench occupied from ten until four and not approached, a copy of a notice bought for a penny in a room off the fish market, two hundred and eleven of one nine-line sheet of a drowned city since the twenty-seventh of April, and a woman who says she is not going to stop.** **The far bench occupied from ten until four and not approached.** The notice copied for a penny out of the ship's own hand and not out of the yard's book; a copyist of about fifty-nine with twenty-two years in that room who has never once asked what she is copying, who says out loud that she has made **two hundred and eleven** copies of **one nine-line sheet about a chamber in a drowned city** since the twenty-seventh of April — **a different sheet from the naval notice, of which not one word is in it, and the two share nothing but a counter and a penny** — and is not going to stop, **because a copyist who stops copying is a copyist who has admitted she copied something, and a trade can be put in a schedule and a confession cannot**; who names the four buyers she knows, of which the first is this yard on the twenty-seventh of April at about half past two, a man of about thirty-two with split hands who did not sit down, and the third is a woman who bought nine at a penny each on the ninth of May and carried them out in a cloth against her chest with both arms, and about whom nobody in that market knows what a person does with nine. **Cost:** two hundred and eleven copies in a town and a reason for not stopping that this yard has no argument against, and a book in her own hand that this yard did not ask to see. **Opens:** the woman with the cloth, which is not followed and will not be.**

**Chapter 309 — Sunday the twenty-third of May. A man of about thirty-one of the third lay with a Crown chart in a bag who says the can was there in the spring, a herring skipper of about fifty-one with a stove out of his wheelhouse, and a season of nine marks that is not going to be paid.** A man of about thirty-one says three things in a market in about nine people's hearing: **the can upon a stake at the outer marks is not on a Crown chart of about nine years standing, not on the outer marks, not on the mole-head approach and not in the approaches table at the back; it was there in the last week of March; and he is not going to say where he got the chart and nobody in that market is going to ask him.** The chart is not opened. **The holder of that writ stood in that market for about four minutes and asked him nothing, and said out loud that he used the four minutes on the not-asking and that this is the second time in a month and both of them were the same mistake.** Elias Voke comes ashore and says he is not going to ask this yard for a stove, and that the harbor authority's clerk took his letter and said he had no season, **and that it has been the best answer he has ever been given and the worst one he has ever been given.** **Cost:** a second time in five volumes this yard could do nothing about a man who is cold, and no figure that tells unable and decided-not-to apart.

**Chapter 310 — Monday the twenty-fourth of May. The fifty-fifth time that wall came down, the forty-ninth charge walked on none, a leaf with four columns read back, three true things said about three different objects, and a man of about thirty-four of a screw-steam packet out of Bramble Strait standing on the mole steps with a copied line in his hand.** **THE CLOSE. The wall bare for the fifty-fifth time and the cistern charged and walked on none, being the forty-ninth, and the far bench occupied from ten until four and not approached.** The read-back: **forty-two entries in the first column at the hour of it and forty-three at the close; one hundred and thirty-nine lines in the second at the hour of it and one hundred and forty-three at the close; none in the third; thirty-four in the fourth and thirty-five at the close, and the thirty-fifth is the last thing in that book.** **The three true things, about three different objects, all true, none improved on: the notice, which has two true sentences four lines apart and which the holder of that writ will not reduce to a design in either direction; the copies, which cannot be made a conspiracy, and the third part of which is him saying out loud that he wanted to put the woman with a cloth in front of a person with a pen and is not going to, because the only reason he could do it is that he is good at finding things out; and the wall, said from the top of the steps by a man of about sixty who was not asked up there, being that about eleven people on this coast cannot read a notice and that nobody in this town has said one word about that in seven days.** Then the last thing done that day: **a man of about thirty-four of a screw-steam packet out of Bramble Strait comes ashore at about five, does not go up the hill, and reads out loud on the stone at the top of the mole steps, in about nine people's hearing, the line off a sheet folded in four, being *a page in a shed on the mole at Morrow Quay, in a drawer, fourth of five*;** and says he came four days up a coast to say it out loud rather than have it found quietly, that he is not going to say who he works for, and that he does not work for anybody, and that the market four days up this coast would not take the sheet down — **and that the line is on that wall and in a drawer in this shed and on about sixty boats, and it is on those boats because a yard printed it in its first year with a place for every copy of itself on the face of it, and he has not got anything against that.** **Cost:** the yard is findable in ten days, and nobody in it wrote, sent, thanked or asked. **Opens:** the volume.

### What the batch paid, in one line

**NOTHING CAME INTO THAT YARD IN TEN DAYS AND ONE HUNDRED AND SIXTY-NINE PENCE WENT OUT, SO THE BAG WENT FROM TWO HUNDRED AND SIXTY-ONE PENCE TO NINETY-TWO, AND THE FIGURE THE BAG IS SHORT OF THE WAGE OF FOUR MARKS WENT FROM THREE HUNDRED AND SEVENTY-NINE PENCE TO FIVE HUNDRED AND FORTY-EIGHT, AND THIRTY WEEKS STOOD FALLEN DUE WITH NO ENVELOPE. THE BATCH'S OTHER COSTS WERE A CHANDLER'S NINE WORDS, A COPYIST'S TWO HUNDRED AND ELEVEN COPIES OF A SHEET ABOUT A DROWNED CITY, ELEVEN BOATS LYING OFF INSIDE THEIR OWN HARBOR LINE, A PLASTERER'S TWO MARKS AND ONE SHILLING AGAINST EIGHT MARKS, NINE DAYS ON A CHEST, A MOLE-HEAD LIGHT OUT IN OCTOBER, AND A STOVE ON AN AFTERDECK. NONE OF THEM WAS PAID BY THIS YARD AND NONE OF THEM WAS OWED TO IT. AND THE ONE THING THIS BATCH DID NOT PAY IS THE VOLUME'S OWN GOVERNING SENTENCE, WHICH A MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY OF THE FIRST LAY HAS THREE THINGS ABOUT AND DID NOT SAY IN A SHOP.**

## Volume 07, Batch 0002 (Chapters 311–320) — complete. Movement 2, a thing that cannot be declared, and a harbor that begins to arrange itself wrongly. **MOVEMENT 2'S TURN IS PAID AND THE VOLUME'S NEW-VOLUME QUESTION HAS NOW BEEN ASKED ONCE AND IS NOT ANSWERED. THE VOLUME IS NOT CLOSED.**

**BUILT OUT OF `outline/volume-07.md`'s § *Escalation sequence* MOVEMENT 2, § *Volume climax*, § *Concrete resolution*, § *New-volume question*, § *Final chapter image* and § *Planned relationship and power change*, ITS THIRTY-ONE LOCKS AND ITS TWO LETTERED ONES, AND THE FOUR STATE FILES AS THEY STOOD AFTER BATCH 0001. NO CARD FILE EXISTS FOR ANY VOLUME 07 BATCH AND NONE WAS INVENTED, AND `outline/batches/` IS UNCHANGED.**

**Batch 0002 as written, AS RE-MEASURED AFTER THE REPAIR OF 28 SEPTEMBER 2026 AGAINST `logs/batch-0002.review.log`: 32,804 words across Chapters 311 to 320** (Ch311 2,987 · Ch312 3,015 · Ch313 3,170 · Ch314 3,333 · Ch315 3,149 · Ch316 3,339 · Ch317 3,312 · Ch318 3,180 · Ch319 3,278 · Ch320 4,041), measured with `wc -w` including headings, against the 2,200–3,200 guideline. **TEN CONSECUTIVE DAYS FROM TUESDAY THE TWENTY-FIFTH OF MAY TO THURSDAY THE THIRD OF JUNE, ONE DAY TO A CHAPTER, NO DAY MISSING AND NO DAY IN TWO CHAPTERS; THE ONE DAY THAT HAD BEEN IN TWO CHAPTERS WAS PUT BACK INTO ONE, SEE `state/continuity.md` ITEM 1.0a. FIVE CHAPTERS ARE INSIDE THE GUIDELINE AND FIVE ARE PAST IT, being 314, 316, 317, 319 and the close at 320; the shape as first written was nine inside and one past, and the change is the cost of the repair and is recorded rather than trimmed away: item 11 of the review asked for a line of dated work the scene did not do in three second-column entries and those three entries took six hundred and eighteen words, and Chapter 315 took sixty-nine on the repair of the day, and no scene was cut to put a number back. Chapter 320 is eight hundred and forty-one words past the guideline, which is the same shape as Chapter 300 at eight hundred and three past it and Chapter 310 at one thousand two hundred and thirty-five past it; it carries the read-back of four columns with the batch's own count on the face of it, three statements and the whole standing inventory once, which is the same shape as Chapters 300, 280 and 310. NO SCENE WAS CUT AND NO COST WAS DROPPED AND NO DATE MOVED TO BRING ANY CHAPTER INSIDE A NUMBER, AND NO PERCENTAGE OF APPARATUS IS QUOTED, because the last figures quoted under that name were measured on different definitions. EVERY COUNTED FIGURE IN THE TEN CHAPTERS COUNTS: the governing sentence is twenty-one words, the Friday sentence sixteen, the yes-and-no sentence twelve, the sentence with a trade in the front of it fifteen, and the captain sentence sixty-three.**

### The chapter summaries

**Chapter 311 — Tuesday the twenty-fifth of May. The thirty-first week with no envelope, a man of about sixty of the first lay back into a shop he said he would come back into, sixteen words he counted on Friday and twenty-one he counted this morning, and a restriction that has arrived in a sentence.** A wage Tuesday, four marks not paid, the column does not move, **thirty-one weeks fallen due.** He had said on the twenty-first that he would come back on Monday; Monday came, he was on the mole steps at half past five and did not come into the shop, and he was in it on the twenty-fifth from ten until about one with the two unlit candles. **He says out loud, in about nine people's hearing, a sentence of twenty-one words, being *a restriction that arrives in a sentence and not in a boat is a thing a man cannot be angry at*, and then the second half of it unnumbered, being *and a man who cannot be angry at it will be angry at something else by Friday*.** He has counted the first four times and says so; he counted the sixteen-word sentence of the twenty-first in his own house on the Friday night. **He says out loud that he did it in nine people's hearing on purpose and noticed in about the fourth word that he was doing the very thing he had warned about, and did it anyway**, because a true thing said only to a man in his own house is worth nothing to anybody else. A chandler says a man of fifty-one with a stove on his afterdeck stood at his counter on the Saturday for a quarter of an hour and bought no candle at fourpence, and that he has watched this three times on this coast and has never been able to tell afterwards which was which. Nobody in that yard sent anybody. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 312 — Wednesday the twenty-sixth of May. Nine people in a fish market, a man of about forty-three reading the fourth line off his own form out loud, a man of about fifty who is not going to choose and says so, and a yard that does not advise.** A man of about forty-three has had a form in his coat since Monday and reads the fourth line out loud twice, being *whether she carries material which is the subject of an unsettled claim*, and asks a man of about fifty what to put in the box. **A yes is a handing over and a no is a lie** — twelve words, counted out loud by the woman of about fifty-eight, who says she has a figure for a candle and none for a yes. Two reasons given out loud, and a statement that he will go out on the Friday with the box empty and that **that is the absence of a plan and not a plan.** The yard is asked in a market for an opinion and refuses in five parts, and says out loud that **a party with no paper in this country has one advantage, which is that nothing anybody does for it is a party to anything**, and is not giving it up for a box in a market. Nine boats of the third lay lying off where there were eleven on the twenty-fourth. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 313 — Thursday the twenty-seventh of May. The fiftieth charge walked on none, two men and a clerk and a window onto the west basin, a quarter owed by that office and said out loud in a room, and a day that does not go round again.** The cistern charged and walked on none, being the fiftieth. **Two men go into the office of that harbor authority separately, are not in the room together, and neither is sent by anybody.** The man of fifty-one of the eighteenth is in there twenty minutes. Then a man of about thirty-six of the third lay puts a form on the clerk's table, is refused in **the same three things in the same order**, is told the clerk will not unsay or improve on the four words of the eighteenth, and is given **a fourth thing that is not one of the three, being that the clerk is not going to hold it.** The man of thirty-six says out loud that he has eleven weeks of a season, has not been out since the seventeenth, is not going out on the Friday, and **that that is a day**, and says it to the room and not to the clerk; and the clerk says out loud, unprompted, that he has been owed a quarter by his own office since the first of April and has not mentioned it to a living soul, and that **nothing has been taken off him.** The holder of that writ is on the west basin walk for a quarter of an hour and does not go in, gives the man of thirty-six the price of a boat and the rule of the first of May, and **writes down that the man did not ask why.** Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 314 — Friday the twenty-eighth of May. A woman of thirty back up a hundred and forty feet of stone with a crate, one question about a round of glass in a drawer, one mark and eightpence into a bag that had nothing come into it since the fifth, and an anchor fluke out of nine feet of mud inside the harbor line.** **The far bench occupied from ten until four and not approached.** **Mara Venn comes back up the mole on her own back with a crate because she said she would and not because anybody asked her to, and asks one question, once, about the round of glass in a drawer four days up that coast, being *who is the second hand on it*, and it is not answered**: one address in this country, no second, a party of one is not a custody. **He says the sentence of the twenty-fourth of April out loud and then says it is about the sheet on that wall and is not about her drawer and is not going to be used about it, including by her.** Then at about half past two, at a box on the corner of the mole, **one mark and eightpence, being one hundred and sixty-eight pence, comes into that yard's own book for the first time since the fifth of May**: a boat of about thirty tons with a man in her for a day, hired by a boat's owner of about fifty-five for his own account and his own purpose, to get his own hook and chain out of about nine feet of mud off the west quay inside that harbor's own line. **It is said out loud that it is not salvage, not a claim, not a claim window, not a charter and not a way of getting past anything, and that the notice governs leaving the port and passing the line and does not govern one shackle of chain in nine feet of mud.** The man in her is **Cobb Terrell, about forty-one, of that harbor's own second lay, a hired hand, and he gives his name out loud and it went in**; the boat's owner says all five things to him and not the yard, before the painter went and not after. **At about half past three a broken stud on the end of that chain comes through the mud and takes him across the top of the left foot.** He does not go in the water. Nobody from that yard was in the boat or on the water at any hour of that day.

**Chapter 315 — Saturday the twenty-ninth of May. Eight boats of the third lay lying off, a sentence carried four days and changed in the front of it, a trade named in the first four words, and fourpence a candle at a counter that is not going to sell any more of them.** **A man of about thirty-six of the third lay says out loud in a fish market in about nine people's hearing a sentence of fifteen words, being *the chandler of the front street put a sentence into this harbor on Tuesday morning*, and says that he carried it four days and put those four words on the front of it at his own table on purpose on the Thursday night**, and says the whole of the twenty-one-word sentence and the *by Friday* half of it, and says out loud that it is the day after Friday and that there is nothing in this harbor he can be angry at that has a hull on it. The chandler listens twice, says nothing, shuts his door and does not open it again that day. **On the Sunday his wife counts the sentence out loud at fifteen words at her own counter, says the first four of them are her trade and that she did not put them there, and says her husband is not going to sell that man candles again, and that a candle at that harbor is fourpence and has been for as long as she has been in that shop, and that about sixty of them between that day and the end of June is four shillings, and that it is a price of fourpence and it is going to be a price of nought.** **Nobody in that yard went and told the chandler to sell to him, nobody went and told the chandler not to, and nobody went and told the man he was wrong or that he was right**, and the holder of that writ says out loud that he is not going to write four shillings against a man's anger and is not going to put a candle in a wheelhouse out of a bag, and gives the reason: the moment this yard does that it is a party to a quarrel between two men in this harbor, and it has paid that three times this month for other people and is not paying it a fourth time for itself. A man of about thirty-four says again on that mole in about nine people's hearing that nobody in that yard has gone and told one of the eleven people he can name what is on that wall. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 316 — Sunday the thirtieth of May. The far bench occupied from ten until four and not approached, a man in a blue coat of about fifty with a book at the head of a slip, the standing reason given out loud before the man answered, the answer given once and not improved on, and two marks, seven shillings and fourpence said in a market on purpose.** **Cobb Terrell says his own figure out loud twice, of his own accord, being two marks, seven shillings and fourpence, being four hundred and eight pence, ten days without weight on that foot and fourteen off the water, and says he is saying it in public because a thing said once in a room does not stay in a room.** **A man in a blue coat, about fifty, with a book, who is not an officer and has no rank and is not named and is a working man and not a face of anything, asks him one question: what is that yard doing on this harbor. The holder of that writ gives the standing reason out loud BEFORE the man answers** — a man in a boat this yard has paid is a man this yard can be asked about, the rule of the first of May, given to the asker and not to the man asked — **and then Cobb Terrell answers: *I am not going to tell you what you should have said*. Nobody improved on it and nobody argued with it and about nine people heard it, and the holder of that writ gives out loud on the mole the reason it is said a second time: a man who has a sentence when it is easy and a better sentence when it is not has a chosen sentence, and that is what the first of May is for.** The man in the blue coat then says three things out loud, and **none of the three is a threat and none is a lie**: he has not got a room with men in it deciding this and has got a notice, a form, a desk, a line from a mole-head to a can upon a stake, a master who will not tick a box and a man in an office who cannot write anything down; he does not know whether that is right and would like to go on not knowing; **and a restriction that gets a man's paper into a room he cannot reach is a bad instrument, which he does not say is a false one, and he has been using it for nine days and has watched a boat of about thirty tons lie off that mole with a stove out of her wheelhouse while it was bad.** He goes off in a dinghy and is not followed. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 317 — Monday the thirty-first of May. The fifty-sixth time that wall came down, the fifty-first charge walked on none, sixty-three words said at a mole head in about nine people's hearing and counted by the man who said them, and one mark and eightpence at a box on a corner at four o'clock.** The wall bare for the fifty-sixth time, the cistern charged and walked on none, being the fifty-first. **A man of about sixty of the first lay says out loud at a mole head in about nine people's hearing a sentence of sixty-three words, being: *the day a man in this harbor works out that it is cheaper to be angry at a boat than it is at a sentence, this harbor will get a captain, and it will be one of ours, and it will be a good one, and it will be a man who is angry and who is telling the truth about being angry*.** He counted it four times and says so, and says that being careful with it for ten days has got a chandler's counter shut, a man of thirty-six with no candles and a man of forty-one with a bone in his foot. **A man of about thirty-eight begins to say, in about nine people's hearing, that this harbor wants somebody to speak for it, and the holder of that writ stops him about four words from finishing — in front of all of them, so that the unfinished thing is on a mole and not in a drawer — and says he is not going to tell the man what should come next and is not going to refuse it either, and that he is going to leave a space on that mole where it was going to go, and that not answering a question and refusing it are two different things and he is not going to be both of them on the same morning.** He is asked for a name, has not got one, will not go and look for one, and says this is the third thing and the correct number of times to ask a question in a harbor is three and not four. He leaves the two unlit candles on the stone and the chandler comes up for them. **At about four in the afternoon the man of about thirty-six hires the same boat for the same figure to shift about nine tons of salt off a lighter inside that harbor's own line**, and the man in her is about twenty-six and gives his name and it went in. **The question is not answered, no person on that mole named a body and no person on that mole proposed one.**

**Chapter 318 — Tuesday the first of June. The thirty-second week with no envelope, nine figures on the back of a slate and one of them that moved, three hundred and seventy-eight pence against four hundred and fifty, a sheet dated the twenty-ninth of May off a boat, and a mole-head light that was out in October.** A wage Tuesday, four marks not paid, **thirty-two weeks fallen due**, the next on Tuesday the eighth of June for the thirty-third. **A sheet dated the twenty-ninth of May, a bill of an agent at Ternwatch and not a quotation, is read out loud at the end stall in about forty people's hearing, and ONE FIGURE CHANGES: a barrel of lamp oil, three hundred and seventy-eight pence in February being thirty-one shillings and sixpence, and four hundred and fifty pence today being thirty-seven shillings and sixpence, a rise of seventy-two pence, being six shillings exactly.** It goes on the slate the day it is quoted to her and not the day she heard it, with the February figure left visible above it and both dates on the board. **The other eight figures do not move, and the small bottle of physic at fourteen pence with *none in this harbor* against it does not move, on purpose.** She says nobody in this harbor set it, that a man in a city four hundred miles off wrote it on the twenty-ninth and has never been to this quay, and that she has said *I have none* to about two hundred people since the ninth of April of whom about forty had money, **and that from today she can say a figure instead of a sentence and that she has lost the only thing she had.** She says a barrel at the February figure means the mole-head light is out in October, and that seventy-two pence is a fifth more oil and a light is not a fifth more light. **A man of about twenty-six with a hod of lime says in about nine people's hearing that the man of thirty-six is not to be trusted with anything, and the man he says it to does not answer him and looks at the two figures on the slate instead.** A doctor of about fifty-five says at that table that a man in a bed at Ashtide has been on days since the seventh of May and that today is the twenty-fifth day. **The holder of that writ refuses to say the rise is the notice and refuses to say it is not, and says so out loud.** Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 319 — Wednesday the second of June. A man of sixty-one back on the water with a bandaged right hand, a bonesetter of about sixty down a hill he does not go up, two marks, seven shillings and fourpence out of a bag with four hundred and twenty-eight in it, and twenty pence left.** **The far bench occupied from ten until four and not approached. Tobias Wren is on the crown of that mole at about half past eight in the morning with a bandaged right hand and a boat's hook in his left, and nobody sent for him, nobody met him, nobody said good morning, and he worked.** **He is not this yard's injury and not this yard's sacrifice and is not the man in a boat this yard hired, and the yard knows the difference and has entered it and is not going to spend him.** At about two in the afternoon a bonesetter of about sixty, in that trade thirty-one years and about four hundred things, comes down that hill because he does not go up one, and **two marks, seven shillings and fourpence, being four hundred and eight pence, goes out of that yard's own book at a counter over a chandler's** — the largest figure to leave that book since the ninth of April past, and a shade under twice what the same trade charged on the eighth of May past for a hand. He does not say no, says he does not want to know the trade's business, and says that a man angry at a foot is a man who has not yet worked out what else he is angry at. **The holder of that writ puts the whole price of the fortnight out loud: a party with no instrument may be hired by a private person for a private purpose, one mark and eightpence is the price of it, a yard that has three marks standing to a man of Ashtide since the eighth of August past, and seven pence standing to its own shipwright, and eight marks and a day of a boat standing to that harbor's own bellwright, has gone and been the thing that moves things inside a blockade, and the answer is that it is exactly like the other things and is not going to be made into anything else, and a man in a boat this yard has paid is a man this yard can be asked about, which was true before the money was taken and is exactly as true after a bonesetter and is four hundred and eight pence more expensive.** A woman of no office asks one question, being where the money came from, is answered in full, and says two words and asks nothing else.

**Chapter 320 — Thursday the third of June. The fifty-second charge walked on none, a leaf with four columns read back in full with the count on the face of it, the whole standing inventory once and nowhere else, and the figure the bag is short of the wage of four marks.** **THE CLOSE. The cistern charged and walked on none, being the fifty-second and the last of the three in this batch. THE READ-BACK: fifty-two entries in the first column at the hour of it and fifty-three at the close, one for every day from the twenty-fifth of May to the second of June; one hundred and seventy-four lines in the second at the hour of it and one hundred and seventy-seven at the close, being one hundred and forty-three, thirty-one and three; none in the third, with the boy's hand at the head of it and the name beside it not said again; forty-four in the fourth at the hour of it and forty-five at the close, and the forty-fifth is the last thing in that book. THE SECOND COLUMN'S FIGURE IS THAT LEDGER'S OWN AND IS NOT A COUNT OF PARAGRAPHS, AND THE FIRST COLUMN'S FIFTY-TWO AND THE FOURTH COLUMN'S FORTY-FOUR, BOTH AT THE HOUR OF THE READ-BACK, ARE PARAGRAPH COUNTS AND ARE CHECKABLE. THE BATCH'S OWN COUNT IS PRINTED ON THE FACE OF IT: ONE wall wipe, THREE cistern charges walked on none, TWO wage Tuesdays, THIRTY-TWO WEEKS FALLEN DUE.** **The three things, about three different objects, all true, none improved on: the notice and the price, which this yard will not put in one sentence in either direction; the harbor, which is seven boats of the third lay where there were eleven on the twenty-fourth, a chandler's counter shut to one man, and a second Tuesday on which nothing happened; and the yard itself, which took two hires and paid a bonesetter and cannot tell afterwards whether that was a good afternoon or a bad one.** Then the whole standing inventory, once, and nowhere else, and the three sentences that are the house's own. Nothing in, nothing out.

### The repair of 28 September 2026 against `logs/batch-0002.review.log`, recorded once

**ELEVEN ITEMS WERE RAISED AND ELEVEN WERE REPAIRED. NO CHAPTER WAS REWRITTEN, NO SCENE WAS CUT, NO DATE WAS MOVED, NO COST WAS DROPPED AND THE PLANNED PLOT IS UNCHANGED; WHAT MOVED IS FOUR ARITHMETIC FIGURES, FOUR SELF-REFERENTIAL CONSTRUCTIONS, ONE DAY THAT WAS IN TWO CHAPTERS, TWO APPARATUS PARAGRAPHS AND THREE LEDGER LINES.** Item 1: Chapter 319's total out since the nineteenth of May was four hundred and eight pence and is five hundred and seventy-seven. Item 2: the bonesetter's four hundred and eight was called a third of the two hundred and eight of the eighth of May and is a shade under twice, in the chapter and in three state files. Item 3: three hundred and eighty pence was spelled two marks, four shillings and eightpence in Chapters 314, 315 and 316 and is two marks and five shillings. Item 4: the fourth column's read-back figure was printed as forty-five in Chapter 320, in this file and in the Batch 0003 hand-off and is forty-four, forty-five being the close. Item 5: four self-referential constructions are gone from Chapters 319 and 320, three of them *a yard that has spent six volumes* and one of them *not this volume's injury*, and the load is carried by the standing unpaid list instead. Item 6: the Batch 0003 hand-off named the third of June a Wednesday and it is a Thursday, and a stray *Tuesday the fifth* is gone from the same paragraph. Item 7: Chapter 315 staged the chandler's wife's count on the Sunday, which is Chapter 316's day, and it now falls on the Saturday the twenty-ninth, and Chapter 316's *on the Saturday following* is now *on the Saturday last, being the twenty-ninth*, which agrees with its own first column. Item 8: Chapters 312 and 318 carried one bold apparatus paragraph each and now carry two, split on the same seam the other eight use, with no word added or removed. Item 9: twenty pence was called a fifth of a candle at fourpence and buys five of them. Item 10: Chapter 320 used the noun *condition* and does not. Item 11: three second-column entries, in Chapters 314, 316 and 317, now carry a dated line of work the scene did not do, which is what the second column is for and which the entries as first written did not do. **THE REVIEWER'S METHOD NOTE IS CARRIED AT `state/continuity.md` ITEM 9.10: A LOOSE SEARCH FOR *SELL* HITS CHAPTER 315'S OWN SUBTITLE, *Not Going to Sell Any More of Them*, WHICH IS THE CHANDLER'S COUNTER AND NOT AMBROSE SELL, SO A SWEEP FOR THE NAMES HAS TO BE ANCHORED.**

### What the batch paid, in one line

**TWO PRIVATE HIRES INSIDE THE HARBOR LINE CAME IN AT THREE HUNDRED AND THIRTY-SIX PENCE, BEING TWO MARKS AND ONE SHILLING AND FOURPENCE, AND A BONESSETTER'S BILL WENT OUT AT FOUR HUNDRED AND EIGHT PENCE, BEING TWO MARKS, SEVEN SHILLINGS AND FOURPENCE, SO THAT THE BAG WENT FROM NINETY-TWO PENCE TO TWENTY PENCE, BEING ONE SHILLING AND EIGHTPENCE, AND THE FIGURE THE BAG IS SHORT OF THE WAGE OF FOUR MARKS WENT FROM FIVE HUNDRED AND FORTY-EIGHT PENCE TO SIX HUNDRED AND TWENTY, BEING THREE MARKS, ELEVEN SHILLINGS AND EIGHTPENCE, AND THIRTY-TWO WEEKS STOOD FALLEN DUE WITH NO ENVELOPE. THE BATCH'S OTHER COSTS WERE A FOURTH LINE OF A DECLARATION THAT A YES ANSWERS AND A NO LIES ABOUT; A CHANDLER'S COUNTER SHUT TO ONE MAN AND FOURPENCE A CANDLE HE WILL NOT BUY; A MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-ONE OFF THE WATER FOR FOURTEEN DAYS; A DAY LOST BY A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SIX WHO SAID IT OUT LOUD IN A ROOM HE WAS NOT TALKING IN; A QUARTER OWED BY AN OFFICE SAID OUT LOUD IN A ROOM WITH A WINDOW; FOUR HUNDRED AND EIGHT PENCE FOR A FOOT; A BARREL OF LAMP OIL UP SIX SHILLINGS FOUR HUNDRED MILES OFF AND NOT COMING BACK DOWN; THE DOOR IN THE SIXTH LINE OF A NOTICE; AND THE FACT THAT A YARD WHICH HAS SPENT SIX VOLUMES REFUSING TO BE AN INSTRUMENT OF ANYBODY IS NOW THE THING THAT MOVES THINGS INSIDE A BLOCKADE, AND THE ANSWER IS THAT IT IS EXACTLY LIKE THE OTHER THINGS. NONE OF THESE COSTS WAS OWED TO THIS YARD. AND THE ONE THING THIS BATCH PAID THAT IT DID NOT CHOOSE IS THE VOLUME'S OWN NEW-VOLUME QUESTION, WHICH WAS ASKED ONCE, AT A MOLE HEAD, IN ABOUT NINE PEOPLE'S HEARING, BY A MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY WHO HAS COUNTED IT FOUR TIMES, AND WAS NOT ANSWERED, AND A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHTEEN WAS STOPPED ABOUT FOUR WORDS FROM PROPOSING ONE.**

## Volume 07, Batch 0003 (Chapters 321–330) — complete. Movement 3, what a chart does not have on it. **THE VOLUME'S MIDPOINT IS WRITTEN AND ITS TURN IS PAID. THE VOLUME IS NOT CLOSED.**

NINE CHAPTERS ARE INSIDE THE GUIDELINE AND ONE IS PAST IT,** being Chapter 330, the close, at nine hundred and fifty-four words past it, which is the same shape as Chapter 310 at one thousand two hundred and thirty-five past it, Chapter 320 at eight hundred and forty-one past it and Chapter 300 at eight hundred and three past it, and it carries the read-back of four columns with the batch's own count on the face of it, three statements, the foot of the mole's steps, and the whole standing inventory once. **THE THREE HUNDRED AND SIXTY-EIGHT WORDS THE REPAIR ADDED TO THE CLOSE ARE PAID FOR A SCENE THE CHAPTER CLAIMED ON ITS OWN OPENING LINE AND DID NOT WRITE, AND NO SCENE WAS CUT AND NO COST WAS DROPPED TO BRING ANY CHAPTER INSIDE A NUMBER, AND NO PERCENTAGE OF APPARATUS IS QUOTED.** The whole standing inventory is carried in Chapter 330 and nowhere else, and the read-back is carried in Chapter 330 and nowhere else. **NO STAGE IS TAKEN IN THIS BATCH; THE STORM-READER STAGE IS TAKEN IN MOVEMENT 5 ONLY, IN SHARED WORK, WITH A CREW, AT A COST, AND NOT BEFORE. ### The chapter summaries

**Chapter 321 — Saturday the fifth of June. Four old books in a drawer in that shed, a bag going past a slip, and the soundings named as of a coast that has moved.** Cobb Terrell down on a crutch, eight days without weight, carrying word of a man of thirty-one with a chart in a bag. Four vessel books — *Morrow Lass*, *Gull's Rest*, *Tern's Debt*, a stitched book — laid out unopened past the first page. The man with the bag goes past, asked nothing. **First price in the first column before any use: the soundings are of a coast that has moved, no figure for how far to be approached.** Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 322 — Sunday the sixth of June. A man going past a shop with an empty basket, eleven weeks of a season, a spelling is a year, and the second price named before anybody says yes.** The chandler's wife counts sixty candles at fourpence being four shillings and calls the nought the thing that sits on the counter. On the crown the man of thirty-six says his father spelled one rock three ways in three years. **Second price in the first column: the route can be used only if a crew gives up the Ternwatch road inside the line, about eleven tons to a lighter, for the season; nobody has said yes.** Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 323 — Monday the seventh of June. The fifty-seventh wipe, the fifty-third charge, a Crown chart of about nine years looked at from a doorway, and two copies neither of which this yard has held.** The wall bare for the fifty-seventh time; the cistern charged and walked on none, the fifty-third. From the reeve's office doorway, about a minute, the framed chart: Bramble Strait with soundings both shores and nothing across the middle. The man of fifty with a form unfilled at the slip, asked nothing. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 324 — Tuesday the eighth of June. The thirty-third week with no envelope, three nots unasked, a foot carrying weight, and two books opened with three spellings.** A wage Tuesday, four marks not paid, **thirty-three weeks fallen due**; the boy not on the stone, the nots against this day not asked about. Cobb without a crutch, the ten days up with the seventh, in the water on the eleventh. *Morrow Lass* and *Gull's Rest* opened: *Skur Deep, Scurr Deepe, Skere Depe*, nine, nine and a half, eight and three quarters fathom, eleven, seventeen, twenty-three years past. A difference in two books, not a truth about a sea. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 325 — Wednesday the ninth of June. About nine people in a market, a boat's name in a man's mouth, a bag not opened, four minutes stood on purpose.** The man of thirty-one names her himself — *Ashtide Rose*, coaster out of Ashtide, about forty tons, nine years his, chart bought fair at Ternwatch six years past with no storm route on it — bag shut, not opened, not asked for. The yard stands four minutes on purpose and asks nothing, as on the twenty-third of May. *Tern's Debt* opened: *Skower Deep, Skour Deap*, nineteen years past. The two men of about thirty-one told apart. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 326 — Thursday the tenth of June. The fifty-fourth charge walked on none, the fourth book opened, four books holding one water under eight spellings, and Tobias Wren looked at for a minute and not asked.** The cistern charged and walked on none, the fifty-fourth. The stitched book opened: *Skorr Depe, Skor Deep, the depe off the Skore*, twenty-seven and nine years past. On the crown the holder looks at the man of sixty-one a minute and does not go over. The second seam a foot and seven inches where it was on the nineteenth of May, ladder in another man's book, not Fisk. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 327 — Friday the eleventh of June. About nine people in a market again, a father's book read out loud, and fourteen days off the water up.** Cobb walks plainly, fourteen days up, back to warps and not in a boat. The man of thirty-six reads his father's square-hand soundings — *Skar Deep, Skarre Depe, Skar Deap*, eight and three quarters, nine, nine and a half fathom, twenty-one, fifteen and eight years past. Five books, one water, disagreeing a quarter to three quarters of a fathom. The man of sixty-one not in the market. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 328 — Saturday the twelfth of June. About forty people in a market, five books and a chart opened by its owner, and a storm route no current chart includes.** The father's three read again; the ship's hand says this yard's eight further spellings out loud; the man of thirty-one lays his own chart open himself, hand on the empty middle water, folds it himself, touched by nobody but him. **A storm route across Bramble Strait; the chart that omits it a Crown chart of about nine years this harbor holds two copies of, neither held by this yard.** The man of sixty-one on the crown all morning, not in the market, not sent for. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 329 — Sunday the thirteenth of June. About forty people on a crown, four minutes looked at, and Tobias Wren refusing both things he could say.** Five open books on two sailcloths; the opportunity given out loud, nobody asked. He steps forward, looks about four minutes, touches nothing, refuses both things — neither named on any page — and says instead, because he has seen it before: *I have seen that water*, eight words, of *Skorr Depe*. Looked at by forty, asked by none. The holder never went over. Nobody agreed in a room, nobody refused in a room, no stage taken. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 330 — Monday the fourteenth of June. The fifty-eighth wipe, the fifty-fifth charge, the read-back with the count on its face, the whole standing inventory once and nowhere else.** **THE CLOSE. READ-BACK at four: sixty-two, two hundred and five, none, fifty-four; AT CLOSE: sixty-three, two hundred and eight, none, fifty-five. Second column the ledger's own; first's sixty-two and fourth's fifty-four at the hour paragraph counts and checkable. THREE COUNTS ON THE FACE: TWO wipes, THREE charges walked on none, ONE wage Tuesday, THIRTY-THREE WEEKS FALLEN DUE. MONEY: 20 + 0 − 0 = 20.** The route restated book by book; both prices standing; three statements about three objects; the whole inventory once. Nothing in, nothing out.

### What the batch paid, in one line

**NOTHING CAME IN AND NOTHING WENT OUT, SO 20 + 0 − 0 = 20 AND THE BAG STANDS AT TWENTY PENCE, BEING ONE SHILLING AND EIGHTPENCE, AND THE FIGURE SHORT OF THE WAGE STANDS AT SIX HUNDRED AND TWENTY PENCE, BEING THREE MARKS, ELEVEN SHILLINGS AND EIGHTPENCE, WITH THIRTY-THREE WEEKS FALLEN DUE. THE BATCH'S OTHER COSTS WERE THE REVERSAL ITSELF — THE BEST RECORD THIS PARTY HAS EVER BEEN OFFERED ON CONDITION IT STOPS CHECKING IT, NEITHER REFUSED ON THE OLD REASON NOR ACCEPTED FOR FREE; A FOOT MENDED AND A MAN BACK TO WARPS; AND THE STANDING OF FIVE BOOKS HOLDING ONE WATER UNDER ELEVEN SPELLINGS WITH A CHART OF ABOUT NINE YEARS HOLDING NOTHING ACROSS IT. NONE OF THESE COSTS WAS OWED TO THIS YARD, AND NONE WAS PAID TWICE.**

### The chapter summaries

**Chapter 331 — Tuesday the fifteenth of June. The thirty-fourth week, a form on a table with a box not filled, six told to stand by, and the hiding begun.** A wage Tuesday, four marks not paid, **thirty-four weeks fallen due**. The man of fifty signs, dates, leaves the fourth box empty, lays the paper on a table where the clerk will not hold it, asks his third question and is answered no. The *Osprey*, nineteen tons, six men, no second boat, told to stand by. Rowan Teel gives his name; it goes in. The hiding begins with a true answer, uncorrected after. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 332 — Wednesday the sixteenth of June. Seventy tons out past the line, a mark looked at from a crown, three true things.** The coaster out at ten past seven on the paper on the table, watched from a hundred and thirty yards, not followed. The mark looked at at low water, not measured, not touched, first time in twenty days; the ladder-keeper at the steps with a lead, not asked, name to nobody, not Fisk. A boy of seventeen brings three true things and a fourth that is a refusal of a reason. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 333 — Thursday the seventeenth of June. The fifty-sixth charge, six out to the bar and back with nothing, a second refusal kept apart from the first.** The cistern charged and walked on none, the fifty-sixth. The six out to the bar and no further, back with nothing. A man of forty-four asks Tobias Wren to take the boat; he refuses for a hand and an age, asked nothing after. **That refusal is not the thirteenth's; the two are kept apart.** The ladder-keeper speaks one sentence about figure and hour; asked nothing; his name not written down. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 334 — Friday the eighteenth of June. The mark put to the new use, and a figure into a book of the six.** Two of the yard in a borrowed dinghy, half past six to two; fouling read three times, water read at the same moments, agreement inside an inch; four figures read off a page not copied; band wiped where read, seal put back. Written reason at five in the morning, one morning, one boat, one day. The figures into the launch book on a line with the date, six names under, being nine foot on the bar and eight and a half off the foul at four o'clock that afternoon. **The hour the figure applies to is four o'clock in the afternoon and it is one hour throughout the batch.** **The use is new on this coast, settles nothing, is not a stage.** A day's labour spent, unpaid. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 335 — Saturday the nineteenth of June. Twenty pence out for oil, a lamp in a window, a boat on the foul, and the catching.** Twenty pence out, two quarts at ten pence, bag nothing since. A lamp burns two o'clock to midnight, nobody's order. At twenty to six the *Osprey* on the foul, eleven foot over her, six ashore by two boats on their own account; **she was on that ground at the four o'clock hour with the eight and a half over it and the flood then came over her, which is the record in the first column and is not an answer to why.** The figure was right; the boat was on the ground. **Caught by a boy's word in front of a woman: a Tuesday lost, not a record; twenty pence named, neither well spent nor badly.** A man of forty hurt, silent. 20 + 0 − 20 = 0.

**Chapter 336 — Sunday the twentieth of June. Four days and nine hours out and a stove out, a price with no figure for three and none for part of a day, nobody died said flat.** The seventy-tonner in with a stove out of her wheelhouse and a stranger's fender; no page says where she was. **Out since ten past to seven on the Wednesday and in at four on the Sunday, which is four days and about nine hours.** Four days of one man six hundred and seventy-two pence, being four marks, two shillings and eightpence; no figure for part of a day and none invented, no figure for three and none invented, not a penny to this yard. The wreck no salvage and no claim. Rowan at the slip, arm hidden, saying nothing. **Nobody died, said flat, not relieved, comforted by nobody.** Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 337 — Monday the twenty-first of June. The fifty-ninth wipe, the fifty-seventh charge, sixteen pence out of a man's own hand, a name on a seventh line, the use closed.** The wall bare for the fifty-ninth time; the cistern fifty-seventh. The bonesetter comes down the hill on a Monday, as the end-stall woman said on the Sunday, and sets Rowan's forearm; sixteen pence, paid by the man himself. The man of fifty-five: the line staying on, same reason as in May. Rowan writes his name on the seventh line under the nineteenth. **The use closed: four figures and no more, no fifth at any hour of any day.** The yard's own three-figure count on the face: one wipe, two charges so far with one to come, one wage Tuesday so far with a second to fall. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 338 — Tuesday the twenty-second of June. The thirty-fifth week, and a market in which nobody proposed anything.** A wage Tuesday, four marks not paid, **thirty-five weeks fallen due**. About nine people hear the four boats as they stand — the wreck, the hard-line boat, the thirty-ton coaster **of the man of about thirty-six**, and the seventy-tonner four days past the line — and there is no boat; nine figures and none the price of one; a page is not a hull. **Nobody proposed a body, committee, assembly, fleet, wing or guard; the word *network* in no mouth; a man stopped two words into a sentence. Lock L22 holds. This yard took the figure of ninety on the fifteenth, the day the six were told to stand by, so the twenty-second is the eighth day of it.** Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 339 — Wednesday the twenty-third of June. About forty people, a book read aloud by its owners, a man on his own feet saying what he did and what it cost.** The launch book opened by its owners, two lines and six names, the eighteenth's line reading the figures and the hour they were given, the seventh line under the nineteenth reading *out at half past five by the flood*. **The man of about thirty-six of the third lay is NOT the man who asks for the table; that is the man of about thirty-eight, and it is said on the page on the morning of the twenty-third.** The holder stands on the floor of the market and says once: the eighteenth's use, the nineteenth's wreck, the figure right, no instrument to claim her. **Not improved on, comforted by nobody.** The six: a checkable figure cost a boat and an arm. The stall woman: not a different morning. The ladder-keeper eight words; answered *no*. Nothing in, nothing out.

**Chapter 340 — Thursday the twenty-fourth of June. The fifty-eighth charge, the read-back with the count on its face, the whole standing inventory once and nowhere else.** **THE CLOSE. READ-BACK at four: seventy-two, two hundred and forty-three, none, sixty-four; AT CLOSE: seventy-three, two hundred and forty-six, none, sixty-five, the sixty-fifth last. Second column the ledger's own; first's seventy-two and fourth's sixty-four at the hour paragraph counts and checkable. THREE COUNTS ON THE FACE: ONE wipe, THREE charges walked on none, TWO wage Tuesdays, THIRTY-FIVE WEEKS FALLEN DUE. MONEY: 20 + 0 − 20 = 0, BAG NOTHING.** Three statements about three objects; tenth day of the ninety, the figure having been taken on the fifteenth. Nothing in, nothing out.

### What the batch paid, in one line

**TWENTY PENCE OUT AND NOTHING IN, SO 20 + 0 − 20 = 0 AND THE BAG STANDS AT NOTHING, AND THE FIGURE SHORT OF THE WAGE STANDS AT SIX HUNDRED AND FORTY PENCE, BEING FOUR MARKS, WITH THIRTY-FIVE WEEKS FALLEN DUE. THE BATCH'S OTHER COSTS WERE A BOAT OF ABOUT NINETEEN TONS WITH SIX MEN IN HER ON THE BOTTOM OFF THE SOUTH END AND SIX MEN ASHORE WITH NO BOAT; A USE OF THE SECOND SEAM'S MARK IT HAD NEVER BEEN PUT TO, TAKEN ONCE AND CLOSED; A TUESDAY TAKEN FROM A WOMAN OF NO OFFICE AND PAID FOR IN OIL; AND A SENTENCE SAID ON A MAN'S OWN FEET IN ABOUT FORTY PEOPLE'S HEARING THAT NOBODY IMPROVED ON AND NOBODY COMFORTED. NONE OF THESE COSTS WAS OWED TO THIS YARD, AND NONE WAS PAID TWICE.**
